  1153	# ── Daemon ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1154	
  1155	
  1156	class PipelineCoordinator:
  1157	    """Skeleton coordinator daemon: tick → evaluate → log no-op → heartbeat.
  1158	
  1159	    Collaborators are injected for deterministic tests; ``from_env`` /
  1160	    default construction wires the production defaults. The daemon holds
  1161	    no durable state of its own beyond an in-memory work-graph cache STUB
  1162	    that is rebuilt every tick (ADR-0021 §3.2 stateless-across-restarts).
  1163	    """
  1164	
  1165	    def __init__(
  1166	        self,
  1167	        config: CoordinatorConfig,
  1168	        *,
  1169	        engine: DecisionEngine | None = None,
  1170	        mode_selector: ModeSelector | None = None,
  1171	        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
  1172	        heartbeat: HeartbeatWriter | None = None,
  1173	        decision_log: DecisionLog | None = None,
  1174	        capacity_provider: Callable[[], CapacitySnapshot] | None = None,
  1175	        work_graph_provider: Callable[[], WorkGraph] | None = None,
  1176	        action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1177	        cold_start_gateway: ColdStartGateway | None = None,
  1178	        sleep: SleepFn | None = None,
  1179	        bridge_tailer: BridgeEventTailer | None = None,
  1180	        jira_poller: JiraEventPoller | None = None,
  1181	        deduper: EventDeduper | None = None,
  1182	        sprint_replan_handler: SprintReplanHandler | None = None,
  1183	        learning_loop: "Any | None" = None,
  1184	    ) -> None:
  1185	        self._config = config
  1186	        self._engine = engine or DecisionEngine()
  1187	        self._mode_selector = mode_selector or ModeSelector()
  1188	        self._clock = clock
  1189	        self._heartbeat = heartbeat or HeartbeatWriter(config.heartbeat_path, clock=clock)
  1190	        self._decision_log = decision_log or DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir, clock=clock)
  1191	        self._capacity_provider = capacity_provider or self._default_capacity
  1192	        # 29f-3: how the daemon learns the per-tick world. Default yields an
  1193	        # empty WorkGraph (skeleton / idle); 29f-8 injects the JIRA-poll +
  1194	        # bridge-tap builder. Every rule abstains on an empty graph.
  1195	        self._work_graph_provider = work_graph_provider or self._default_work_graph
  1196	        # 29f-3: action layer. Default is shadow (observe-only) so a rule that
  1197	        # fires never mutates JIRA until the real §6 layer / acting mode lands.
  1198	        executor = action_executor or ShadowActionExecutor().execute
  1199	        self._action_executor: ActionExecutor = executor
  1200	        # 29f-6: event sources (ADR §4) — bridge JSONL tail + 60s JIRA poll +
  1201	        # hourly sweep, coalesced through a 5-min deduper. They feed the
  1202	        # per-tick raw events staged for the work-graph builder.
  1203	        self._bridge_tailer = bridge_tailer or BridgeEventTailer(
  1204	            config.bridge_events_path or (config.config_dir / "bridge-events.jsonl")
  1205	        )
  1206	        self._jira_poller = jira_poller or JiraEventPoller(
  1207	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1208	            clock=clock,
  1209	            interval_seconds=config.jira_poll_interval_seconds,
  1210	        )
  1211	        self._deduper = deduper or EventDeduper(
  1212	            window_seconds=config.event_dedupe_seconds,
  1213	            clock=clock,
  1214	        )
  1215	        self._sprint_replan_handler = sprint_replan_handler
  1216	        self._last_sweep_at: datetime | None = self._clock()
  1217	        # 29f-7: cold-start recovery seam. Default wraps the production
  1218	        # jira_dispatch / git / systemctl adapters (lazy + fail-open) in the
  1219	        # observe-only ShadowColdStartGateway (acting=False default, OP-1555);
  1220	        # build_default_coordinator(acting=True) injects the live gateway, and
  1221	        # tests inject a fake so the 4-phase decision tree is verified in
  1222	        # isolation.
  1223	        self._cold_start_gateway: ColdStartGateway = (
  1224	            cold_start_gateway or _default_cold_start_gateway(config)
  1225	        )
  1226	        # 29f-11: learning loop (ADR §10). Default ``None`` keeps the skeleton /
  1227	        # test daemon inert; build_default_coordinator wires the Cognee
  1228	        # write-back + 24h outcome-check + weekly graduation handlers. When
  1229	        # present, every Tier-2 tick is written back within the tick ("within
  1230	        # 60s", Integration AC) and the daily/weekly schedules are gated here.
  1231	        self._learning_loop = learning_loop
  1232	        self._learning_seeded = False
  1233	        self._stop = threading.Event()
  1234	        # Interruptible sleep: default waits on the stop event so SIGTERM
  1235	        # breaks the loop within one poll instead of one tick.
  1260	        29f-6 collects raw source events (bridge tail + 60s JIRA poll +
  1261	        hourly sweep, coalesced) and stages them in ``_pending_events``;
  1262	        29f-8 turns those into the WorkGraph the rules reason over via the
  1263	        injected provider. Until that builder lands, the default provider
  1264	        yields an empty graph so the daemon ticks safely while events are
  1265	        already being collected.
  1266	        """
  1267	        self._pending_events = self._collect_source_events()
  1268	        self._work_graph = self._work_graph_provider()
  1269	        return self._work_graph
  1270	
  1271	    def _current_situation(self) -> tuple[SituationProfile | None, str | None]:
  1272	        """STUB: the (profile, operator-override) the current tick decides on.
  1273	
  1274	        29f-8 derives a :class:`SituationProfile` from the ticket/event under
  1275	        decision (urgency / risk / novelty / reversibility signals — ADR
  1276	        §7.1) and reads any ``coord-mode:*`` operator override label off the
  1277	        ticket. The skeleton has no situation, so it returns ``(None, None)``
  1278	        and the tick stays in the idle :data:`SKELETON_MODE`.
  1279	        """
  1280	        return None, None
  1281	
  1282	    def _collect_source_events(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1283	        events: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1284	        events.extend(self._bridge_tailer.poll())
  1285	        events.extend(self._jira_poller.poll())
  1286	        sweep = self._hourly_sweep_event()
  1287	        if sweep is not None:
  1288	            events.append(sweep)
  1289	        fresh: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1290	        for event in events:
  1291	            if self._deduper.fresh(event):
  1292	                fresh.append(event)
  1293	        return fresh
  1294	
  1295	    def _hourly_sweep_event(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
  1296	        now = self._clock()
  1297	        if self._last_sweep_at is None:
  1298	            self._last_sweep_at = now
  1299	            return None
  1300	        elapsed = (now - self._last_sweep_at).total_seconds()
  1301	        if elapsed < self._config.sweep_interval_seconds:
  1302	            return None
  1303	        self._last_sweep_at = now
  1304	        if self._sprint_replan_handler is not None:
  1305	            try:
  1306	                return self._sprint_replan_handler.run().to_event()
  1307	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - hourly handler must not kill daemon
  1308	                logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] sprint re-plan failed: %s", exc)
  1309	                return {
  1310	                    "source": "timer",
  1311	                    "trigger": "hourly-sprint-replan",
  1312	                    "payload": {
  1313	                        "error": str(exc),
  1314	                        "pickable_count": 0,
  1315	                        "total_free_slots": 0,
  1316	                        "actions": [],
  1317	                        "action_results": [],
  1318	                    },
  1319	                }
  1320	        return {
  1321	            "source": "timer",
  1322	            "trigger": "hourly-sweep",
  1323	            "payload": {
  1324	                "scan": "full-work-graph-anomaly",
  1325	                "elapsed_seconds": elapsed,
  1326	            },
  1327	        }
  1328	
  1329	    def build_context(self) -> DecisionContext:
  1330	        """Assemble the per-tick :class:`DecisionContext`.
  1331	
  1332	        ``mode`` is the *idle* baseline (skeleton when there is no situation);
  1333	        the engine re-selects a real personality mode from ``situation`` +
  1334	        ``mode_override`` before rule evaluation (ADR-0021 §7). ``ticket`` /
  1335	        ``tickets`` expose the WorkGraph slice the Tier-1 rules reason over.
  1336	        """
  1337	        situation, mode_override = self._current_situation()
  1338	        wg = self._work_graph
  1339	        return DecisionContext(
  1340	            now=self._clock(),
  1341	            capacity=self._capacity_provider(),
  1342	            mode=self._mode_selector.select(),
  1343	            situation=situation,
  1344	            mode_override=mode_override,
  1345	            ticket=wg.focal,
  1346	            tickets=dict(wg.tickets),
  1347	        )
  1348	
  1349	    # ── decision-log record (ADR-0021 §3.2 example) ──
  1350	
  1351	    def _build_tick_record(
  1352	        self,
  1353	        result: DecisionResult,
  1354	        action_results: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
  1355	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  1356	        # A no-op tick records ``actions:[]``; only real (non-noop) actions
  1357	        # are serialised into the array (skeleton emits none).
  1358	        actions = [a.to_record() for a in result.actions if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)]
  1359	        dry_run = all(a.dry_run for a in result.actions) if result.actions else True
  1360	        record: dict[str, Any] = {
  1361	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1362	            "event": "decision_tick",
  1363	            "engine_version": result.engine_version,
  1364	            "mode": result.mode,
  1365	            "decision_id": result.decision_id,
  1366	            "actions": actions,
  1367	            "reason": result.reason,
  1368	            "dry_run": dry_run,
  1369	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1370	        }
  1371	        # When a personality mode fired (non-idle tick), record the behavior
  1372	        # knobs that steered Tier-1 / Tier-2 so the selected mode is auditable
  1373	        # in the shadow/decision log (ADR-0021 §7.2 + Integration AC).
  1374	        if result.mode_behavior is not None:
  1375	            record["mode_behavior"] = {
  1376	                "tier1_dominant": result.mode_behavior.tier1_dominant,
  1377	                "tier2_policy": result.mode_behavior.tier2_policy,
  1378	                "llm_context_hops": result.mode_behavior.llm_context_hops,
  1379	                "llm_lessons_window": result.mode_behavior.llm_lessons_window,
  1380	            }
  1381	        # 29f-3: name the firing rule + tier (ADR Appendix C) and the action
  1382	        # layer's per-action outcomes — only when a rule actually fired, so a
  1383	        # plain no-op tick keeps the skeleton's minimal record shape.
  1384	        if result.rule_name is not None:
  1385	            record["rule_name"] = result.rule_name
  1386	        if result.tier is not None:
  1387	            record["tier"] = result.tier
  1388	        # 29f-6: the Tier-2 LLM-consultation block (ADR Appendix C) — only on a
  1389	        # tick that actually consulted (or degraded) the LLM, so a Tier-1 / no-op
  1390	        # tick keeps its minimal record shape and ``budget_cap`` events stay
  1391	        # grep-able + replayable for the daily-spend rebuild.
  1392	        if result.llm_consultation is not None:
  1393	            record["llm_consultation"] = dict(result.llm_consultation)
  1394	        if action_results:
  1395	            record["action_results"] = action_results
  1396	        return record
  1397	
  1398	    # ── cold-start 4-phase recovery (ADR §3.3 + §9 L6) ──
  1399	
  1400	    def _log_cold_start(self, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  1401	        """Append a cold-start decision-log record, stamping ts/pid/engine."""
  1402	        base: dict[str, Any] = {
  1403	            "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1404	            "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1405	            "pid": os.getpid(),
  1406	        }
  1407	        base.update(record)
  1408	        self._decision_log.append(base)
  1409	
  1410	    def startup(self) -> ColdStartReport:
  1410	    def startup(self) -> ColdStartReport:
  1411	        """Run the 4-phase cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3). Returns a report.
  1412	
  1413	        Order: Startup-1 (infra) → L6 crash-recovery (ADR §9 L6: "after Phase
  1414	        Startup-1") → Startup-2 (JIRA reconcile) → Startup-3 (stale sweep) →
  1415	        Startup-4 (log steady-state entry). If Startup-1 cannot bring the
  1416	        expected infra up, the daemon halts startup (``phase1_halted=True``)
  1417	        and does NOT proceed — entering the loop with degraded infra is worse
  1418	        than not running (ADR §3.3 Startup-1 failure path). :meth:`run` reads
  1419	        the report to decide whether to enter the loop.
  1420	        """
  1421	        report = ColdStartReport()
  1422	        self._log_cold_start({"event": COLD_START_BEGAN_EVENT})
  1423	        self._startup_1_infra(report)
  1424	        if report.phase1_halted:
  1425	            self._log_cold_start(
  1426	                {"event": COLD_START_COMPLETE_EVENT, "halted_at": "startup-1",
  1427	                 "still_down": report.still_down_units}
  1428	            )
  1429	            return report
  1430	        self._l6_crash_recovery(report)
  1431	        self._startup_2_reconcile(report)
  1432	        self._startup_3_sweep(report)
  1433	        self._startup_4_enter_loop(report)
  1434	        self._log_cold_start(
  1435	            {
  1436	                "event": COLD_START_COMPLETE_EVENT,
  1437	                "started_units": report.started_units,
  1438	                "crash_recoveries": len(report.crash_recoveries),
  1439	                "reconciled": len(report.reconciled),
  1440	                "swept": len(report.swept),
  1441	            }
  1442	        )
  1443	        return report
  1444	
  1445	    def _startup_1_infra(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1446	        """Startup-1: verify infra; start any down unit; halt if still down."""
  1447	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1448	        audit = gw.audit_infra()
  1449	        for unit in audit.down:
  1450	            ok = gw.start_unit(unit)
  1451	            (report.started_units if ok else report.still_down_units).append(unit)
  1452	            self._log_cold_start(
  1453	                {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "unit": unit,
  1454	                 "action": "start_unit", "ok": ok}
  1455	            )
  1456	        # Re-verify after starts: a unit that refused to start (or died again)
  1457	        # leaves the infra degraded → @-operator + halt (do not enter loop).
  1458	        if report.still_down_units:
  1459	            recheck = gw.audit_infra()
  1460	            report.still_down_units = list(recheck.down)
  1461	        if report.still_down_units:
  1462	            report.phase1_halted = True
  1463	            gw.mention_operator(
  1464	                "OP",  # infra-level alert is not ticket-scoped
  1465	                "[cold-start Startup-1] coordinator could not bring up "
  1466	                f"{report.still_down_units}; halting startup rather than "
  1467	                "entering the loop with degraded infra (ADR-0021 §3.3).",
  1468	                urgency="high",
  1469	            )
  1470	            self._log_cold_start(
  1471	                {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 1, "action": "halt",
  1472	                 "still_down": report.still_down_units}
  1473	            )
  1474	
  1475	    def _l6_crash_recovery(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1476	        """L6 (ADR §9): replay last 24h decision log; resume/complete/rollback.
  1477	
  1478	        A ``shutdown_began`` with no matching ``shutdown_complete`` (paired by
  1479	        ``decision_id``) means the daemon crashed mid-drain. For each such
  1480	        crash we look at the immediately-preceding tick: if it executed a real
  1481	        (non-dry-run) action that was never confirmed complete, we resolve it
  1482	        per action kind — idempotent relabel/transition are *completed*
  1483	        (re-issued safely), file_ticket is effectively irreversible so we
  1484	        *escalate*, everything else is a logged no-op. Every resolution writes
  1485	        a ``crash_recovery_applied`` record (ADR §9 L6).
  1486	        """
  1487	        records = self._read_recent_decision_log()
  1488	        began: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
  1489	        completed: set[str] = set()
  1490	        last_real_action_tick: dict[str, Any] | None = None
  1491	        for rec in records:
  1492	            event = rec.get("event")
  1493	            if event == "decision_tick" and not rec.get("dry_run", True) and rec.get("actions"):
  1494	                last_real_action_tick = rec
  1495	            elif event == "shutdown_began":
  1496	                began[str(rec.get("decision_id", ""))] = rec
  1497	                # Snapshot which real-action tick preceded this drain.
  1498	                rec["_preceding_real_tick"] = last_real_action_tick
  1499	            elif event == "shutdown_complete":
  1500	                completed.add(str(rec.get("decision_id", "")))
  1501	        for drain_id, drain_rec in began.items():
  1502	            if drain_id in completed:
  1503	                continue  # clean shutdown — nothing to recover
  1504	            tick = drain_rec.get("_preceding_real_tick")
  1505	            resolutions = self._resolve_interrupted_actions(tick)
  1506	            entry = {
  1507	                "event": CRASH_RECOVERY_EVENT,
  1508	                "crashed_drain_id": drain_id,
  1509	                "resolutions": resolutions,
  1510	            }
  1511	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  1512	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1513	
  1514	    def _resolve_interrupted_actions(self, tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1515	        """Map each interrupted action of a crashed tick to a recovery step."""
  1516	        if not tick:
  1517	            return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "no in-flight real action before crash"}]
  1518	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1519	        out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1520	        for action in tick.get("actions", []):
  1521	            kind = action.get("kind")
  1522	            target = action.get("target", "")
  1523	            if kind in (ACTION_RELABEL, ACTION_TRANSITION):
  1524	                # Idempotent → safe to complete (re-issue) on recovery.
  1525	                if kind == ACTION_TRANSITION and action.get("params", {}).get(
  1526	                    "to_status"
  1527	                ) == "Under Review":
  1528	                    gw.transition_under_review(target)
  1529	                resolution = "completed"
  1530	            elif kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
  1531	                # Not safely reversible/repeatable — surface to operator.
  1532	                gw.mention_operator(
  1533	                    target,
  1534	                    f"[cold-start L6] crash interrupted a file_ticket action on {target}; "
  1535	                    "verify the dependency ticket exists and was not double-filed.",
  1536	                    urgency="medium",
  1537	                )
  1538	                resolution = "escalated"
  1539	            else:
  1540	                resolution = "noop"
  1541	            out.append({"kind": kind, "target": target, "resolution": resolution})
  1542	        return out
  1543	
  1544	    def _read_recent_decision_log(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1545	        """Return decision-log records from the last :data:`L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS`.
  1546	
  1547	        Reads the day-partitioned JSONL files spanning the window (today + the
  1548	        days it reaches back into), in chronological order, skipping malformed
  1549	        lines so a single torn record never aborts recovery.
  1550	        """
  1551	        now = self._clock()
  1552	        window_start = now - timedelta(hours=L6_REPLAY_WINDOW_HOURS)
  1553	        records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1554	        day = window_start.date()
  1555	        seen_paths: list[Path] = []
  1556	        while day <= now.date():
  1557	            path = self._decision_log.directory / f"{day.isoformat()}.jsonl"
  1558	            if path.exists():
  1559	                seen_paths.append(path)
  1560	            day = day + timedelta(days=1)
  1561	        for path in seen_paths:
  1562	            try:
  1563	                lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
  1564	            except OSError:
  1565	                continue
  1566	            for line in lines:
  1567	                if not line.strip():
  1568	                    continue
  1569	                try:
  1570	                    rec = json.loads(line)
  1571	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
  1572	                    continue
  1573	                if isinstance(rec, dict):
  1574	                    ts = record_ts(rec)
  1575	                    if ts is None or ts >= window_start:
  1576	                        records.append(rec)
  1577	        return records
  1578	
  1579	    def _startup_2_reconcile(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1580	        """Startup-2: reconcile each interrupted (進行中) ticket (ADR §3.3).
  1581	
  1582	        Decision tree per ticket with no live runner:
  1583	          (b) Gerrit change exists + mergeable      → transition Under Review
  1584	          (a) feature branch has commits             → mark resumable, leave
  1585	          (c) no commits + stale (>2× CLI timeout)   → revert To Do (clear claim)
  1586	          (d) no commits + not yet stale (ambiguous) → @-operator, leave alone
  1587	        A ticket whose runner is still alive is left untouched (it's handling
  1588	        it). (b) is checked before (a): a mergeable change means the work is
  1589	        *finished* and only missed its transition, which beats re-running it.
  1590	        (c) vs (d) hinges on staleness so a runner that only just started (and
  1591	        hasn't committed yet) is never aggressively reverted out from under.
  1592	        """
  1593	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1594	        for ticket in gw.interrupted_tickets():
  1595	            key = ticket.key
  1596	            if gw.has_live_runner(key):
  1597	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "live-runner", "leave")
  1598	            elif gw.gerrit_change_mergeable(key):
  1599	                gw.transition_under_review(key)
  1600	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "gerrit-mergeable", "under_review")
  1601	            elif gw.branch_has_commits(key):
  1602	                gw.mark_resumable(key)
  1603	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "branch-has-commits", "resume")
  1604	            elif ticket.stale:
  1605	                # No commits, no live runner, long-stale → abandoned work.
  1606	                gw.reset_to_todo(key)
  1607	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "no-commits-stale", "revert_todo")
  1608	            else:
  1609	                # No commits but recent — a runner may have only just started.
  1610	                gw.mention_operator(
  1611	                    key,
  1612	                    "[cold-start Startup-2(d)] interrupted with no commits but "
  1613	                    "recent activity — ambiguous; leaving in place for operator review.",
  1614	                    urgency="medium",
  1615	                )
  1616	                self._record_reconcile(report, key, "ambiguous", "operator")
  1617	
  1618	    def _record_reconcile(
  1619	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, classification: str, action: str
  1620	    ) -> None:
  1621	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 2, "ticket": key,
  1622	                 "classification": classification, "action": action}
  1623	        report.reconciled.append({"ticket": key, "classification": classification, "action": action})
  1624	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1625	
  1626	    def _startup_3_sweep(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1627	        """Startup-3: drop orphaned claim:* / dead waiting-* + clear assignees."""
  1628	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1629	        plan = gw.stale_sweep_plan()
  1630	        for key, labels in plan.stale_claims.items():
  1631	            for label in labels:
  1632	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  1633	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  1634	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "stale-claim", list(labels))
  1635	        for key in plan.orphan_assignees:
  1636	            gw.clear_assignee(key)
  1637	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "orphan-assignee", [])
  1638	        for key, labels in plan.resolved_waiting.items():
  1639	            for label in labels:
  1640	                gw.remove_label(key, label)
  1641	            self._record_sweep(report, key, "resolved-waiting", list(labels))
  1642	
  1643	    def _record_sweep(
  1644	        self, report: ColdStartReport, key: str, kind: str, labels: list[str]
  1645	    ) -> None:
  1646	        entry = {"event": STARTUP_PHASE_EVENT, "phase": 3, "ticket": key,
  1647	                 "sweep": kind, "labels": labels}
  1648	        report.swept.append({"ticket": key, "sweep": kind, "labels": labels})
  1649	        self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1650	
  1651	    def _startup_4_enter_loop(self, report: ColdStartReport) -> None:
  1652	        """Startup-4: log steady-state entry (the loop itself is run() → run_forever)."""
  1653	        report.entered_loop = True
  1654	        self._log_cold_start(
  1655	            {"event": STEADY_STATE_ENTERED_EVENT, "ts_entered": self._clock().isoformat()}
  1656	        )
  1657	        logger.info(
  1658	            "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start complete — entered steady state at %s",
  1659	            self._clock().isoformat(),
  1660	        )
  1661	
  1662	    def run(
  1663	        self,
  1664	        *,
  1665	        install_signals: bool = True,
  1666	        max_ticks: int | None = None,
  1667	    ) -> int:
  1668	        """Production boot: cold-start recovery, then the steady-state loop.
  1669	
  1670	        Runs :meth:`startup` first (ADR §3.3 — the coordinator is the canonical
  1671	        startup orchestrator). If Startup-1 halted on degraded infra, the loop
  1672	        is NOT entered and 0 ticks are returned. Otherwise hands off to
  1673	        :meth:`run_forever` (Startup-4).
  1674	        """
  1675	        report = self.startup()
  1676	        if report.phase1_halted:
  1677	            logger.error(
  1678	                "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start halted in Startup-1 "
  1679	                "(infra still down: %s) — not entering steady-state loop",
  1680	                report.still_down_units,
  1681	            )
  1682	            return 0
  1683	        return self.run_forever(install_signals=install_signals, max_ticks=max_ticks)
  1684	
  1685	    # ── entrypoints ──
  1686	
  1687	    def run_once(self) -> DecisionResult:
  1688	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  1689	
  1690	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  1860	
  1861	
  1862	def build_default_coordinator(
  1863	    config: CoordinatorConfig | None = None,
  1864	    *,
  1865	    acting: bool = False,
  1866	    action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1867	) -> PipelineCoordinator:
  1868	    """Production wiring: env config, hybrid Tier-1+Tier-2 engine, shadow layer.
  1869	
  1870	    29f-3 shipped the Tier-1 rule engine; 29f-6 fronts it with the Tier-2 LLM
  1871	    consultant (:class:`HybridDecisionEngine`) so a tick that no Tier-1 rule
  1872	    matches (or that the personality mode escalates) consults the claude CLI
  1873	    under a daily budget cap. Constructing the engine logs the loaded rule
  1874	    registry (Deploy AC). The budget guard rebuilds the day's spend from the
  1875	    decision log (ADR §3.2 stateless-across-restarts). The action layer
  1876	    defaults to shadow (observe-only) so this stays safe for the 29f-14 7-day
  1877	    shadow canary. ``acting=True`` is the single switch that permits the main
  1878	    action executor, the hourly sprint re-plan handler, AND the 4-phase
  1879	    cold-start recovery to mutate; ``acting=False`` wires every one of them
  1880	    observe-only (cold-start via :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway`, OP-1555).
  1881	    """
  1882	    config = config or CoordinatorConfig.from_env()
  1883	    if acting and action_executor is None:
  1884	        raise ValueError("acting=True requires an explicit action_executor")
  1885	    if not acting and action_executor is not None:
  1886	        raise ValueError("action_executor requires acting=True")
  1887	    decision_log = DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir)
  1888	    # 29f-11: the learning loop reads + appends to the same decision log; build
  1889	    # it from the log so its daily/weekly schedule survives a restart (§3.2).
  1890	    from backend.agents.learning_loop import build_default_learning_loop
  1891	
  1892	    return PipelineCoordinator(
  1893	        config,
  1894	        engine=build_hybrid_engine(decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir),
  1895	        action_executor=action_executor,
  1896	        cold_start_gateway=_default_cold_start_gateway(config, acting=acting),
  1897	        sprint_replan_handler=build_default_sprint_replan_handler(
  1898	            config_dir=config.config_dir,
  1899	            decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir,
  1900	            capacity_path=config.capacity_path,
  1901	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1902	            shadow=not acting,
  1903	        ),
  1904	        decision_log=decision_log,
  1905	        learning_loop=build_default_learning_loop(decision_log),
  1906	    )
  1907	
  1908	
  1909	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  1910	    """CLI entrypoint. ``--once`` runs a single tick; default runs forever."""
  1911	    import argparse
  1912	
  1913	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1914	    parser.add_argument(
  1915	        "--once",
  1916	        action="store_true",
  1917	        help="run a single tick (heartbeat + one no-op decision) and exit "
  1918	             "(skips cold-start recovery — a diagnostic tick, not a boot)",
  1919	    )
  1920	    parser.add_argument(
  1921	        "--no-cold-start",
  1922	        action="store_true",
  1923	        help="enter the steady-state loop directly, skipping the 4-phase "
  1924	             "cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3) — for local debugging only",
  1925	    )
  1926	    parser.add_argument(
  1927	        "--config-dir",
  1928	        type=Path,
  1929	        default=None,
  1930	        help="override the coordinator config dir (default ~/.config/omnisight/coordinator)",
  1931	    )
  1932	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  1933	
  1934	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  1935	    config = CoordinatorConfig.from_env(config_dir=args.config_dir)
  1936	    coordinator = build_default_coordinator(config)
  1937	    if args.once:
  1938	        coordinator.run_once()
  1939	        return 0
  1940	    if args.no_cold_start:
  1941	        coordinator.run_forever()
  1942	        return 0
  1943	    # Production boot: 4-phase cold-start recovery, then steady-state loop.
  1944	    coordinator.run()
  1945	    return 0

   192	def heartbeat_stale_after_seconds(env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> int:
   193	    """Resolve ``OMNISIGHT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC``; defaults to 300."""
   194	
   195	    e = env if env is not None else os.environ
   196	    raw = e.get("OMNISIGHT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC", "").strip()
   197	    if not raw:
   198	        return DEFAULT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC
   199	    try:
   200	        value = int(raw)
   201	    except ValueError:
   202	        return DEFAULT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC
   203	    return max(1, value)

   206	def touch_heartbeat_file(path: Path | None = None, now: float | None = None) -> Path:
   207	    """Touch the heartbeat file to ``now`` (or wall-clock ``time.time()``).
   208	
   209	    Creates the parent directory if missing — the systemd unit may run as
   210	    a user-level service against ``~/.local/state/omnisight-bridge/`` and
   211	    we cannot rely on the deployer pre-creating the directory.
   212	    """
   213	
   214	    target = path if path is not None else heartbeat_path_from_env()
   215	    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   216	    target.touch(exist_ok=True)
   217	    ts = now if now is not None else time.time()
   218	    os.utime(target, (ts, ts))
   219	    return target

   258	def check_bridge_heartbeat(
   259	    path: Path | None = None,
   260	    stale_after_seconds: int | None = None,
   261	    now: float | None = None,
   262	) -> tuple[bool, float, Path]:
   263	    """Return ``(is_fresh, age_seconds, resolved_path)``.
   264	
   265	    A missing heartbeat file is treated as stale (``is_fresh=False``,
   266	    ``age_seconds=inf``) — the bridge has either never started or its
   267	    install drifted from the configured path; pickup-side callers must
   268	    fail closed in either case.
   269	    """
   270	
   271	    target = path if path is not None else heartbeat_path_from_env()
   272	    stale = (
   273	        stale_after_seconds
   274	        if stale_after_seconds is not None
   275	        else heartbeat_stale_after_seconds()
   276	    )
   277	    current = now if now is not None else time.time()
   278	    try:
   279	        mtime = target.stat().st_mtime
   280	    except FileNotFoundError:
   281	        return False, float("inf"), target
   282	    age = max(0.0, current - mtime)
   283	    return age <= stale, age, target
   284

  2184	    def stream_forever(self) -> None:
  2185	        reap_stale_merger_verify_worktrees(logger=self.log)
  2186	        # SP-B-X-009 — emit one heartbeat before catchup so the file
  2187	        # appears on disk the moment the daemon is up. Without this the
  2188	        # gate could read a missing file during the (potentially minute-
  2189	        # long) startup catchup and trip a false "bridge down" alert.
  2190	        self._touch_heartbeat_file()
  2191	        self._last_heartbeat_file = time.monotonic()
  2192	        # SP-B-X-019 / OP-1077 — start the heartbeat thread BEFORE
  2193	        # catchup. ``startup_catchup`` and the SSH stream read can both
  2194	        # block for minutes during quiet Gerrit periods; without the
  2195	        # thread, ``_maintenance_ticks``'s heartbeat write only fires
  2196	        # when the next stream-event arrives, and the on-disk file
  2197	        # goes stale → runner pickup gate trips a false "bridge_down".
  2198	        # The thread runs ``_touch_heartbeat_file`` on a half-cadence
  2199	        # wall clock so the gate's 5-min stale threshold never trips
  2200	        # while the daemon is healthy. ``stop()`` sets the Event so
  2201	        # shutdown doesn't wait a full cycle.
  2202	        self._start_heartbeat_thread()
  2203	        self.startup_catchup()
  2204	        self.replay_from_cursor()
  2205	        consecutive_failures = 0
  2206	        backoff = 1.0
  2207	        while not self._stop:
  2208	            proc = self.popen_factory(
  2209	                self._ssh_cmd("gerrit", "stream-events"),
  2210	                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
  2211	                stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  2212	                text=True,
  2213	                env=self._ssh_env(),
  2214	            )
  2215	            if proc.stdout is None:
  2216	                raise RuntimeError("stream-events stdout pipe unavailable")
  2217	            for raw_line in proc.stdout:
  2218	                self._maintenance_ticks()
  2219	                payload = parse_stream_line(raw_line)
  2220	                if payload is None:
  2221	                    self.counters.parse_errors += 1
  2222	                    self.log("WARN", "malformed_json_line", err=raw_line.strip()[:500])
  2223	                    continue
  2224	                self.process_stream_event(payload)
  2225	                self.save_event_cursor(payload)
  2226	                if self._stop:
  2227	                    break
  2228	            rc = proc.wait()
  2229	            stderr = proc.stderr.read() if proc.stderr is not None else ""
  2230	            if self._stop:
  2231	                break
  2232	            if self._is_auth_failure(stderr):
  2233	                self.log("ALERT", "gerrit_auth_failed", err=stderr[-500:])
  2234	                raise GerritAuthError("Gerrit SSH auth failed")
  2235	            consecutive_failures += 1
  2236	            self.counters.gerrit_reconnects += 1
  2237	            if consecutive_failures >= self.config.alert_after_failures:
  2238	                self.log(
  2239	                    "ALERT",
  2240	                    "gerrit_stream_reconnects_high",
  2280	    def _touch_heartbeat_file(self) -> None:
  2281	        """SP-B-X-009 — write the on-disk heartbeat for the pickup gate.
  2282	
  2283	        Errors are logged but never raised: a stale heartbeat is *exactly*
  2284	        the failure-mode the pickup gate is designed to detect, so a write
  2285	        failure must not also crash the daemon. The runner side will pick
  2286	        up the stale mtime and notify the operator on its next pickup."""
  2287	
  2288	        path = self.config.heartbeat_file_path or heartbeat_path_from_env()
  2289	        try:
  2290	            touch_heartbeat_file(path)
  2291	        except OSError as exc:
  2292	            self.log(
  2293	                "WARN",
  2294	                "heartbeat_file_write_failed",
  2295	                err=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
  2296	                heartbeat_path=str(path),
  2297	            )
  2298	
  2299	    def _start_heartbeat_thread(self) -> None:
  2300	        """SP-B-X-019 / OP-1077 — start the wall-clock heartbeat thread.
  2301	
  2302	        The thread is a ``daemon=True`` background worker that calls
  2303	        :meth:`_touch_heartbeat_file` every ``heartbeat_file_seconds / 2``
  2304	        (clamped to a 5s floor) regardless of Gerrit event traffic. This
  2305	        keeps the on-disk heartbeat fresh during quiet periods when the
  2306	        SSH stream-events read blocks for minutes — without the thread,
  2307	        the runner-side pickup gate would falsely trip ``bridge_down``.
  2308	
  2309	        ``stop()`` sets ``self._heartbeat_thread_stop`` so the thread
  2310	        observes the Event during its sleep and exits within one
  2311	        check interval rather than waiting a full cadence.
  2312	
  2313	        Half-cadence (``heartbeat_file_seconds / 2``) keeps the stale
  2314	        window safely below the runner gate's ``OMNISIGHT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC``
  2315	        threshold even if one tick fires late.
  2316	
  2317	        Idempotent: if the thread is already alive (e.g. ``stream_forever``
  2318	        is restarted by an in-process test), this method is a no-op so
  2319	        we don't accumulate threads.
  2320	        """
  2321	        if self._heartbeat_thread is not None and self._heartbeat_thread.is_alive():
  2322	            return
  2323	        self._heartbeat_thread_stop.clear()
  2324	        cadence = max(5.0, float(self.config.heartbeat_file_seconds) / 2.0)
  2325	
  2326	        def _run() -> None:
  2327	            # First write happens at startup via stream_forever; loop
  2328	            # waits a full cadence before the first repeated write.
  2329	            while not self._heartbeat_thread_stop.wait(cadence):
  2330	                try:
  2331	                    self._touch_heartbeat_file()
  2332	                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — must never crash the daemon
  2333	                    # _touch_heartbeat_file already swallows OSError; this
  2334	                    # catches anything else (e.g. mocked-test sentinels).
  2335	                    self.log(
  2336	                        "WARN",
  2337	                        "heartbeat_thread_write_failed",
  2338	                        err=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
  2339	                    )
  2340	
  2341	        thread = Thread(
  2342	            target=_run,
  2343	            name="bridge-heartbeat",
  2344	            daemon=True,
  2345	        )
  2346	        self._heartbeat_thread = thread
  2347	        thread.start()
  2348	
  2380	
  2381	    def _is_auth_failure(self, blob: str) -> bool:
  2382	        return any(marker.lower() in blob.lower() for marker in AUTH_FAILURE_MARKERS)

  2385	def build_bridge(agent_class: str = "subscription-claude") -> GerritJiraBridge:
  2386	    client = jira_dispatch.make_client(agent_class)
  2387	    auth = jira_dispatch._GERRIT_AUTH_BY_CLASS.get(
  2388	        agent_class,
  2389	        jira_dispatch._GERRIT_AUTH_BY_CLASS["subscription-claude"],
  2390	    )
  2391	    user, key_path = auth
  2392	    return GerritJiraBridge(
  2393	        client,
  2394	        BridgeConfig(
  2395	            agent_class=agent_class,
  2396	            gerrit_user=user,
  2397	            gerrit_key_path=key_path,
  2398	        ),
  2399	    )

  2407	async def _run_with_db_pool(agent_class: str = "subscription-claude") -> None:
  2408	    """Run the bridge with an explicit pool lifecycle.
  2409	
  2410	    The stream-events daemon runs outside FastAPI's lifespan context,
  2411	    but proactive merger audit writes still use ``backend.audit.log``.
  2412	    Initialise the process-global pool here before any stream event can
  2413	    spawn merger work.
  2414	
  2415	    OP-1190: also pre-warm ``GerritClient``'s account-resolution cache.
  2416	    Per-event handler threads call ``asyncio.run(...)`` and therefore
  2417	    run in a fresh event loop; the asyncpg pool created above is bound
  2418	    to THIS (main) loop and cannot be acquired from those worker loops.
  2419	    Pre-warming here lets ``_proactive_merger_check`` and
  2420	    ``_ai_reviewer_check`` resolve the Gerrit account synchronously from
  2421	    the cache without touching the pool from a different loop.
  2422	    """
  2423	    dsn = _resolve_pg_dsn()
  2424	    if not dsn:
  2425	        raise BridgeFatalError(
  2426	            "Gerrit/JIRA bridge requires a Postgres DSN for audit logging"
  2427	        )
  2428	    await db_pool.init_pool(dsn)
  2429	    from backend.gerrit import GerritClient
  2430	    await GerritClient.prewarm_for_daemon()
  2431	    bridge = build_bridge(agent_class)
  2432	    # OP-1196 phase 3b — one-shot scan of pre-existing conflicts that
  2433	    # never received a live stream event. After this returns, live
  2434	    # `_handle_patchset_created` events take over normally. Backfill
  2435	    # failure does NOT block the main stream loop — it's logged and
  2436	    # swallowed via the bridge.backfill_existing_conflicts internal
  2437	    # try/except, returning 0 on any error.
  2438	    try:
  2439	        bridge.backfill_existing_conflicts()
  2440	    except Exception as exc:                              # pragma: no cover
  2441	        # Defensive — the method already swallows its own errors, but if
  2442	        # something genuinely catastrophic escapes (e.g., missing
  2443	        # subprocess module) we still want stream_forever to run.
  2444	        structured_log(
  2445	            "ERROR", "backfill_unexpected_exception",

     1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2	# scripts/sync_sora_bridge.sh — OP-798 Phase A.
     3	#
     4	# Keeps the long-lived /home/user/sora-bridge/ working tree in sync with
     5	# origin/develop and bounces the gerrit-jira-bridge.service ONLY if a
     6	# file the daemon actually imports changed. Wired via the
     7	# sora-bridge-sync.timer (5 min cadence).
     8	#
     9	# Why a separate clone exists at all:
    10	#   The bridge daemon must run from a tree that is NEVER touched by the
    11	#   runner / dev worktrees (which constantly switch branches and reset
    12	#   state). Sharing the main checkout would race the daemon against
    13	#   every runner tick. Today's failure mode (OP-798) was the inverse:
    14	#   isolation works, but with no automated sync the clone drifted 233
    15	#   commits behind and silently kept running pre-OP-743 code for ~7h.
    16	#
    17	# Behaviour (in order):
    18	#   1. Refuse to run if BRIDGE_DIR is missing or not a git repo.
    19	#   2. If working tree is dirty (operator debug edits) → log + exit 0.
    20	#      Do NOT touch the tree. Dirty state is a SAFE skip, not a failure.
    21	#   3. git fetch origin develop. fetch failure → counted as a failure.
    22	#   4. If HEAD == origin/develop → reset failure counter, exit 0.
    23	#   5. Otherwise compute diff, decide whether any RELEVANT file changed
    24	#      (i.e. would actually affect the daemon's runtime behaviour).
    25	#   6. git pull --ff-only. Non-FF → log + exit non-zero (no force).
    26	#   7. If relevant changed → systemctl --user restart $BRIDGE_UNIT and
    27	#      bump the on-disk restart counter. Else → log "synced_no_restart".
    28	#   8. After 3 consecutive sync failures emit a P0 line to
    29	#      operator_notifier (defence-in-depth; the watchdog T2 catches the
    30	#      same case on a different signal).
    31	#
    32	# The only side-effects on a healthy run are (a) a fast-forward pull,
    33	# (b) optionally one systemctl --user restart, and (c) appending to the
    34	# log + state files. Everything else is informational.
    35	#
    36	# Env overrides (all optional — defaults match the production layout):
    37	#   BRIDGE_DIR     default /home/user/sora-bridge
    38	#   BRIDGE_REMOTE  default origin
    39	#   BRIDGE_BRANCH  default develop
    40	#   BRIDGE_UNIT    default gerrit-jira-bridge.service
    41	#   SYNC_LOG       default /home/user/work/sora/logs/bridge/sync.log
    42	#   SYNC_STATE     default /home/user/work/sora/logs/bridge/sync-state.json
    43	#   SYNC_DRY_RUN   default 0 (1 = log decisions, skip pull + restart)
    44	#
    45	# Exit codes:
    46	#   0  no-op, dirty-skip, synced-no-restart, restart-success
    47	#   2  unrecoverable config error (missing dir / not a repo)
    48	#   3  fetch / pull failure (counted toward the consecutive-failure budget)
    49	#   4  systemctl restart failure (counted)
    50	#
    51	# JSON log records use {ts, event, ...} so journald readers and the
    52	# operator_notifier tail can grep by event= without extra parsing.
    53	
    54	set -uo pipefail
    55	
    56	BRIDGE_DIR="${BRIDGE_DIR:-/home/user/sora-bridge}"
    57	BRIDGE_REMOTE="${BRIDGE_REMOTE:-origin}"
    58	BRIDGE_BRANCH="${BRIDGE_BRANCH:-develop}"
    59	BRIDGE_UNIT="${BRIDGE_UNIT:-gerrit-jira-bridge.service}"
    60	SYNC_LOG="${SYNC_LOG:-/home/user/work/sora/logs/bridge/sync.log}"
    61	SYNC_STATE="${SYNC_STATE:-/home/user/work/sora/logs/bridge/sync-state.json}"
    62	SYNC_DRY_RUN="${SYNC_DRY_RUN:-0}"
    63	SYNC_FAIL_ALERT_THRESHOLD="${SYNC_FAIL_ALERT_THRESHOLD:-3}"
    64	
    65	# RELEVANT_FILES: any change touching one of these paths (exact match,
    66	# repo-relative) triggers a daemon restart. Docs / tests / unrelated
    67	# backend code do NOT. Keep this list tight — every entry here is a
    68	# restart trigger and restarts cost ~5s of stream-events downtime.
    69	#
    70	# Why these five:
    71	#   gerrit_jira_bridge.py     — daemon entrypoint logic
    72	#   jira_dispatch.py          — JIRA transition + comment helpers
    73	#   db.py                     — DB session / engine config
    74	#   git_accounts.py           — Gerrit/JIRA cred lookup at bridge start
    75	#   run_gerrit_jira_bridge.py — the actual ExecStart target
    76	RELEVANT_FILES=(
    77	    "backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py"
    78	    "backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py"
    79	    "backend/db.py"
    80	    "backend/git_accounts.py"
    81	    "scripts/run_gerrit_jira_bridge.py"
    82	)
    83	
    84	mkdir -p "$(dirname "$SYNC_LOG")" "$(dirname "$SYNC_STATE")"
    85	
    86	_now() { date -u +"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"; }
    87	
    88	# Emit a single-line JSON record to both stderr (→ journald via the
    89	# .service unit) and to $SYNC_LOG. event= is always first so grep is
    90	# trivial.
   210	fi
   211	
   212	cd "$BRIDGE_DIR"
   213	
   214	# ── 2. Skip if working tree is dirty ───────────────────────────────
   215	# Operator debug edits (live patches, prints, ad-hoc instrumentation)
   216	# live in the working tree. We MUST NOT clobber them. Skip + warn.
   217	if ! git diff --quiet || ! git diff --cached --quiet; then
   218	    _log sync_skipped_dirty path "$BRIDGE_DIR"
   219	    # A dirty tree is intentional operator state, not a sync failure.
   220	    # Reset consecutive_failures so a long debug session doesn't tip
   221	    # the script into spurious P0 alerts.
   222	    _reset_failure
   223	    _state_write "$CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES" "$RESTARTS" "$LAST_SHA" "$LAST_ALERT_AT"
   224	    exit 0
   225	fi
   226	
   227	# ── 3. Fetch ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   228	if ! git fetch --quiet "$BRIDGE_REMOTE" "$BRIDGE_BRANCH" 2>/tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.fetch.err; then
   229	    _record_failure "fetch_failed" "$(tr -d '\n' </tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.fetch.err | head -c 240)"
   230	    rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.fetch.err
   231	    exit 3
   232	fi
   233	rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.fetch.err
   234	
   235	LOCAL_SHA="$(git rev-parse HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo "")"
   236	REMOTE_SHA="$(git rev-parse "$BRIDGE_REMOTE/$BRIDGE_BRANCH" 2>/dev/null || echo "")"
   237	
   238	if [[ -z "$LOCAL_SHA" || -z "$REMOTE_SHA" ]]; then
   239	    _record_failure "rev_parse_failed" "local=$LOCAL_SHA remote=$REMOTE_SHA"
   240	    exit 3
   241	fi
   242	
   243	# ── 4. Already in sync ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   244	if [[ "$LOCAL_SHA" == "$REMOTE_SHA" ]]; then
   245	    _log sync_no_change head "$LOCAL_SHA"
   246	    _reset_failure
   247	    LAST_SHA="$LOCAL_SHA"
   248	    _state_write "$CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES" "$RESTARTS" "$LAST_SHA" "$LAST_ALERT_AT"
   249	    exit 0
   250	fi
   251	
   252	# ── 5. Decide whether any relevant file changed ────────────────────
   253	CHANGED_PATHS_RAW="$(git diff --name-only "$LOCAL_SHA" "$REMOTE_SHA" 2>/dev/null || true)"
   254	RELEVANT_HIT=""
   255	while IFS= read -r changed; do
   256	    [[ -z "$changed" ]] && continue
   257	    for rel in "${RELEVANT_FILES[@]}"; do
   258	        if [[ "$changed" == "$rel" ]]; then
   259	            RELEVANT_HIT+="$changed "
   260	            break
   261	        fi
   262	    done
   263	done <<< "$CHANGED_PATHS_RAW"
   264	RELEVANT_HIT="${RELEVANT_HIT% }"
   265	
   266	# ── 6. Fast-forward pull (no merge, no rebase) ─────────────────────
   267	if [[ "$SYNC_DRY_RUN" == "1" ]]; then
   268	    _log sync_dry_run from "$LOCAL_SHA" to "$REMOTE_SHA" relevant_changed "${RELEVANT_HIT:-none}"
   269	    _reset_failure
   270	    _state_write "$CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES" "$RESTARTS" "$LAST_SHA" "$LAST_ALERT_AT"
   271	    exit 0
   272	fi
   273	
   274	if ! git pull --ff-only --quiet "$BRIDGE_REMOTE" "$BRIDGE_BRANCH" 2>/tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.pull.err; then
   275	    _record_failure "pull_not_fast_forward" \
   276	        "$(tr -d '\n' </tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.pull.err | head -c 240)"
   277	    rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.pull.err
   278	    exit 3
   279	fi
   280	rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.pull.err
   281	
   282	POST_SHA="$(git rev-parse HEAD)"
   283	_log sync_pulled from "$LOCAL_SHA" to "$POST_SHA"
   284	LAST_SHA="$POST_SHA"
   285	
   286	# ── 7. Restart only if a relevant file changed ─────────────────────
   287	if [[ -n "$RELEVANT_HIT" ]]; then
   288	    if systemctl --user restart "$BRIDGE_UNIT" 2>/tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.restart.err; then
   289	        RESTARTS=$((RESTARTS + 1))
   290	        _log sync_restarted unit "$BRIDGE_UNIT" relevant "$RELEVANT_HIT" \
   291	            restart_count "$RESTARTS"
   292	        _reset_failure
   293	        _state_write "$CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES" "$RESTARTS" "$LAST_SHA" "$LAST_ALERT_AT"
   294	        rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.restart.err
   295	        exit 0
   296	    else
   297	        _record_failure "systemctl_restart_failed" \
   298	            "$(tr -d '\n' </tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.restart.err | head -c 240)"
   299	        rm -f /tmp/.sync_sora_bridge.restart.err
   300	        exit 4
   301	    fi
   302	fi
   303	
   304	_log sync_no_restart_needed from "$LOCAL_SHA" to "$POST_SHA" \
   305	    reason "no_relevant_files_changed"
   306	_reset_failure
   307	_state_write "$CONSECUTIVE_FAILURES" "$RESTARTS" "$LAST_SHA" "$LAST_ALERT_AT"
   308	exit 0

     1	"""Database persistence layer.
     2	
     3	SQLite path via aiosqlite for the legacy dev flow (no
     4	``OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`` set). Production runs against PostgreSQL
     5	through :mod:`backend.db_pool` — every domain helper in this module
     6	takes an explicit ``conn`` argument so pool-acquired connections and
     7	SQLite connections share the same call surface.
     8	
     9	Phase-3 Step C.2 (2026-04-21): the PG-path compat shim
    10	``backend.db_pg_compat.PgCompatConnection`` has been retired. The
    11	prod entry point is now the lifespan-managed ``db_pool.init_pool``;
    12	``db.init()`` below only opens the SQLite dev DB. If a
    13	``OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`` is set, callers are expected to be
    14	pool-native (all domain helpers are), and ``db.init()`` short-circuits.
    15	"""
    16	
    17	from __future__ import annotations
    18	
    19	import json
    20	import logging
    21	import re
    22	from pathlib import Path
    23	from typing import Any
    24	
    25	import aiosqlite
    26	import sqlalchemy as sa
    27	from alembic.ddl.base import AddColumn as _AlembicAddColumn
    28	from sqlalchemy.dialects import sqlite as _sqlite_dialect_mod
    29	
    30	from backend.db_context import (
    31	    tenant_insert_value,
    32	    tenant_where_pg,
    33	)
    34	
    35	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    36	
    37	# FX.1.9: dialect-aware DDL rendering for the in-process SQLite migrator
    38	# below. We render ALTER TABLE statements via SQLAlchemy / alembic's DDL
    39	# compiler instead of f-string interpolation so identifier quoting and
    40	# type/default rendering go through the dialect's escape logic. The
    41	# migration list is hardcoded in source (no user-controlled identifiers),
    42	# but the audit row asked us to take the f-string out of the loop on
    43	# principle — these are the rails the rest of the codebase already uses
    44	# for schema operations (alembic ``op.add_column``).
    45	_SQLITE_DIALECT = _sqlite_dialect_mod.dialect()
    46	_SQLITE_PREPARER = _SQLITE_DIALECT.identifier_preparer
    47	_IDENT_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
    48	# SQLite-accepted ON DELETE / ON UPDATE actions; reject anything else
    49	# rather than splice an attacker-controlled string into DDL even though
    50	# every current call passes a hardcoded literal.
    51	_FK_ACTIONS = {"CASCADE", "SET NULL", "SET DEFAULT", "RESTRICT", "NO ACTION"}
    52	
    53	
    54	def _validate_ident(name: str) -> str:
    55	    if not _IDENT_RE.match(name):
    56	        raise ValueError(f"Unsafe SQL identifier: {name!r}")
    57	    return name
    58	
    59	
    60	def _render_add_column(table: str, column: sa.Column) -> str:
    61	    """Render ``ALTER TABLE <t> ADD COLUMN <c> ...`` via the SQLite dialect.
    62	
    63	    Uses alembic's ``AddColumn`` DDL construct so identifier quoting,
    64	    type rendering, and default-clause escaping all go through the
    65	    SQLAlchemy compiler instead of f-string composition. SQLite has no
    66	    ``ALTER TABLE ADD CONSTRAINT`` form, so any ``ForeignKey`` on
    67	    ``column`` is appended as an inline ``REFERENCES`` clause built
    68	    through the dialect's ``IdentifierPreparer`` (alembic's AddColumn
    69	    intentionally drops FK because most other dialects need a separate
    70	    ADD CONSTRAINT round-trip — for SQLite the inline form is the only
    71	    option).
    72	    """
    73	    _validate_ident(table)
    74	    _validate_ident(column.name)
    75	    if column.table is None:
    76	        # FK / DDL bookkeeping wants a parent Table; a throwaway one is fine.
    77	        sa.Table(table, sa.MetaData(), column)
    78	    sql = str(_AlembicAddColumn(table, column).compile(dialect=_SQLITE_DIALECT))
    79	
    80	    fk_parts: list[str] = []
    81	    for fk in column.foreign_keys:
    82	        # Use ``target_fullname`` (string form) rather than ``fk.column``
    83	        # so we don't trigger SQLAlchemy's MetaData lookup — the parent
    84	        # tables aren't registered in our throwaway MetaData.
    85	        spec = fk.target_fullname
    86	        if spec.count(".") != 1:
    87	            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported FK target: {spec!r}")
    88	        target_table, target_col = spec.split(".", 1)
    89	        _validate_ident(target_table)
    90	        _validate_ident(target_col)
    91	        clause = (
    92	            f"REFERENCES {_SQLITE_PREPARER.quote(target_table)} "
    93	            f"({_SQLITE_PREPARER.quote(target_col)})"
    94	        )
    95	        if fk.ondelete:
    96	            action = fk.ondelete.upper()
    97	            if action not in _FK_ACTIONS:
    98	                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported ON DELETE action: {fk.ondelete!r}")
    99	            clause += f" ON DELETE {action}"
   100	        if fk.onupdate:
   101	            action = fk.onupdate.upper()
   102	            if action not in _FK_ACTIONS:
   103	                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported ON UPDATE action: {fk.onupdate!r}")
   104	            clause += f" ON UPDATE {action}"
   105	        fk_parts.append(clause)
   106	    if fk_parts:
   107	        sql = f"{sql} {' '.join(fk_parts)}"
   108	    return sql
   109	
   110	def _resolve_db_path() -> Path:
   111	    from backend.config import settings
   112	    if settings.database_path:
   113	        return Path(settings.database_path).expanduser()
   114	    return Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "data" / "omnisight.db"
   115	
   116	
   117	def _resolve_pg_dsn() -> str:
   118	    """Return a libpq-style DSN if OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL / DATABASE_URL
   119	    points at a PG host, else empty string.
   120	
   121	    Accepts the usual ``postgresql+asyncpg://user:pw@host/db`` form and
   122	    strips the ``+asyncpg`` qualifier asyncpg doesn't need. Checked in
   123	    ``OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`` → ``DATABASE_URL`` precedence matching
   124	    :mod:`backend.db_url.resolve_from_env`.
   125	    """
   126	    import os
   127	    for key in ("OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL", "DATABASE_URL"):
   128	        url = (os.environ.get(key) or "").strip()
   129	        if not url:
   130	            continue
   131	        low = url.lower()
   132	        if low.startswith(("postgresql://", "postgres://", "postgresql+asyncpg://", "postgres+asyncpg://", "asyncpg://")):
   133	            # asyncpg accepts postgresql://... directly; strip driver qualifier.
   134	            for prefix, canon in (
   135	                ("postgresql+asyncpg://", "postgresql://"),
   136	                ("postgres+asyncpg://", "postgresql://"),
   137	                ("asyncpg://", "postgresql://"),
   138	                ("postgres://", "postgresql://"),
   139	            ):
   140	                if low.startswith(prefix):
   141	                    return canon + url[len(prefix):]
   142	            return url  # already postgresql://
   143	    return ""
   144	
   145	
   146	_DB_PATH = _resolve_db_path()
   147	# Holds the aiosqlite connection for the dev-mode path (no PG DSN).
   148	# Production code does NOT read ``_db`` — it goes through
   149	# ``db_pool.get_pool()``. See Phase-3 Step C.2.
   150	_db: Any = None
   151	
   152	
   153	async def init() -> None:
   154	    """Open the SQLite dev DB and create tables if they don't exist.
   155	
   156	    On SQLite (default, no ``OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL``): create tables
   157	    via CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS, run ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN
   158	    migrations in ``_migrate()``, set WAL pragmas.
   159	
   160	    On Postgres (any ``OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`` pointing at PG): this
   161	    function is a no-op. The PG schema is owned by alembic
   162	    (``alembic upgrade head`` runs at deploy time) and the pool is
   163	    opened by the lifespan handler via ``db_pool.init_pool``. The
   164	    Phase-3 Step C.2 cleanup retired the in-process compat wrapper
   165	    that previously coexisted with the pool on the PG path.
   166	    """
   167	    global _db
   168	    pg_dsn = _resolve_pg_dsn()
   169	    if pg_dsn:
   170	        # Production path: schema + pool are owned elsewhere. Nothing
   171	        # to do here — leave ``_db`` as None so anyone who still
   172	        # reaches for it fails fast with the "not initialized" error.
   173	        logger.info(
   174	            "db.init() no-op on PG path — pool lifecycle owned by "
   175	            "db_pool.init_pool, schema owned by alembic."
   176	        )
   177	        return
   178	
   179	    _DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   180	    _db = await aiosqlite.connect(str(_DB_PATH))

docs/audit/codex-reviews/runner-self-audit-launch.log:7889:OP-1067 added bridge-health as a runner pickup signal. OP-1077 then documents the follow-up: the bridge heartbeat was emitted from `_maintenance_ticks()` inside a loop blocked on `gerrit stream-events`, so quiet Gerrit periods made the heartbeat stale even when the process was alive. The result was that runner pickup would see `bridge_down` and block all work. The workaround was an external `while true; touch heartbeat; sleep 60` loop. I personally saw OP-1077 fail as codex: at 03:56:59 I picked it up, at 03:57:03 pre-sync failed, and later at 04:03:46 my completed verification was blocked by `runner-capability-blocked` because `gerrit_push` was not permitted.

   457	
   458	
   459	# SP-B-X-009 (OP-1067) — C9 bridge-health pickup gate. The Gerrit/JIRA
   460	# bridge daemon owns the change-merged → Published transition; if its
   461	# heartbeat goes stale every freshly picked ticket will eventually wedge
   462	# at Approved waiting for a transition that will never arrive. Running
   463	# this gate before claim_ticket_atomic surrenders the tick early so we
   464	# do not burn a claim slot on work the rest of the pipeline cannot
   465	# finalise. The gate fires ``operator_notifier.notify(Severity.CRITICAL,
   466	# "bridge_down", ...)`` so the alert lands on the on-call channels per
   467	# the standard severity matrix; the JIRA comment side-effect is gated
   468	# behind ``OMNISIGHT_FLEET_HEALTH_CANARY_KEY`` so we don't pollute every
   469	# runner-host's ticket with bridge-down noise.
   470	def _bridge_health_pickup_gate(
   471	    client: jira_dispatch.DispatchClient,
   472	    *,
   473	    check_fn=gerrit_jira_bridge.check_bridge_heartbeat,
   474	    notify_fn=operator_notify,
   475	    comment_fn=jira_dispatch.add_comment,
   476	) -> bool:
   477	    """Return True if the bridge heartbeat is fresh; False otherwise.
   478	
   479	    On stale heartbeat (or missing file) emits a CRITICAL operator
   480	    notification and — only when ``OMNISIGHT_FLEET_HEALTH_CANARY_KEY``
   481	    is set — also posts a ``[runner-bridge-down]`` comment on that
   482	    canary ticket so the symptom is preserved in JIRA for the
   483	    incident retrospective.
   484	    """
   485	
   486	    is_fresh, age_sec, path = check_fn()
   487	    if is_fresh:
   488	        return True
   489	
   490	    age_repr = "missing" if age_sec == float("inf") else f"{age_sec:.0f}s"
   491	    print(
   492	        f"[runner] bridge_down: heartbeat stale at {path} (age={age_repr}); "
   493	        f"skipping pickup",
   494	        file=sys.stderr,
   495	    )
   496	    try:
   497	        notify_fn(
   498	            Severity.CRITICAL,
   499	            "bridge_down",
   500	            message=f"bridge heartbeat stale at {path}; runners blocked",
   501	            context={"heartbeat_path": str(path), "age_sec": age_sec},
   502	        )
   503	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — notifier failure must not wedge the gate
   504	        print(
   505	            f"[runner] bridge_down notify failed: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
   506	            file=sys.stderr,
   507	        )
   508	
   509	    canary_key = os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_FLEET_HEALTH_CANARY_KEY", "").strip()
   510	    if canary_key and not DRY_RUN:
   135	   incident timeline, then `acknowledge(<notification_id>)` the
   136	   `operator_notifier` page so the CRITICAL re-page loop stops.
   137	
   138	## Tuning the gate
   139	
   140	| Env var | Default | Effect |
   141	| --- | --- | --- |
   142	| `OMNISIGHT_BRIDGE_HEARTBEAT_PATH` | `/var/run/omnisight-bridge/heartbeat` | Where the bridge writes and the runner reads. Must agree on both sides. |
   143	| `OMNISIGHT_BRIDGE_STALE_AFTER_SEC` | `300` | Runner-side stale threshold in seconds. Tighten only after observing a steady-state mtime cadence the daemon can honour. |
   144	| `OMNISIGHT_FLEET_HEALTH_CANARY_KEY` | unset | When set to a JIRA key, the gate posts a `[runner-bridge-down]` comment there on each tripped pickup. Leave unset on production runners to avoid ticket-spam; set on a fleet-health canary ticket for incident retros. |
   145	
   146	## Why no JIRA comment by default
   147

  1687	    def run_once(self) -> DecisionResult:
  1688	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  1689	
  1690	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  1691	        skeleton) so callers/tests can assert on it directly.
  1692	        """
  1693	        self._heartbeat.touch()
  1694	        self._refresh_work_graph()
  1695	        self._append_source_event_records()
  1696	        ctx = self.build_context()
  1697	        if self._config.capacity_path is not None:
  1698	            write_capacity_snapshot(ctx.capacity, self._config.capacity_path)
  1699	        result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)
  1700	        # Hand each real (non-noop) action to the action layer. In shadow
  1701	        # mode it only records the would-be execution; the outcomes go into
  1702	        # the decision-log line so a fired rule is observable end-to-end.
  1703	        action_results = [
  1704	            self._action_executor(a, ctx)
  1705	            for a in result.actions
  1706	            if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)
  1707	        ]
  1708	        record = self._build_tick_record(result, action_results)
  1709	        self._decision_log.append(record)
  1710	        self._run_learning_loop(record, result)
  1711	        return result
  1712	
  1713	    def _run_learning_loop(self, record: dict[str, Any], result: DecisionResult) -> None:
  1714	        """Write back a Tier-2 decision + fire any due daily/weekly handler.
  1715	
  1716	        Gated on a wired learning loop (29f-11) so the skeleton / test daemon is
  1717	        unaffected. Write-back happens in-tick so a Tier-2 decision reaches
  1718	        Cognee well within the 60s Integration-AC budget; the daily + weekly
  1719	        schedules are gated by the loop's own cadence (mirrors the hourly
  1720	        sweep). Fail-open: a learning-loop error never breaks a decision tick.
  1721	        """
  1722	        loop = self._learning_loop
  1723	        if loop is None:
  1724	            return
  1725	        try:
  1726	            if not self._learning_seeded:
  1727	                loop.seed_schedule(self._clock())
  1728	                self._learning_seeded = True
  1729	            if result.tier == 2:
  1730	                loop.write_back_decision(record)
  1731	            loop.maybe_run(self._clock())
  1732	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — learning loop is best-effort
  1733	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] learning loop error: %s", exc)
  1734	
  1735	    def _append_source_event_records(self) -> None:
  1736	        for event in self._pending_events:
  1737	            self._decision_log.append(
  1738	                {
  1739	                    "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1740	                    "event": "coordinator_source_event",
  1741	                    "trigger": event.get("trigger"),
  1742	                    "source": event.get("source"),
  1743	                    "ticket_key": event.get("ticket_key"),
  1744	                    "payload": event.get("payload"),
  1745	                    "dry_run": True,
  1746	                    "pid": os.getpid(),
  1747	                }
  1748	            )
  1749	
  1750	    def request_stop(self) -> None:
  1751	        """Ask the steady-state loop to stop after the current tick."""
  1752	        self._stop.set()
  1753	
  1754	    @property
  1755	    def stopping(self) -> bool:
  1756	        return self._stop.is_set()
  1757	
  1758	    def run_forever(
  1759	        self,
  1760	        *,
  1761	        install_signals: bool = True,
  1762	        max_ticks: int | None = None,
  1763	    ) -> int:
  1764	        """Steady-state loop: tick, then sleep one interval, until stopped.
  1765	
  1766	        On stop (SIGTERM via the installed handler, ``request_stop()``, or
  1767	        ``max_ticks`` reached) the loop drains cleanly: writes
  1768	        ``shutdown_began`` + ``shutdown_complete`` to the decision log,
  1769	        then returns the number of ticks executed (ADR-0021 §9 L5).
  1770	
  1771	        ``install_signals`` is False-able so tests can drive the loop from
  1772	        a non-main thread (where ``signal.signal`` would raise).
  1773	        ``max_ticks`` bounds the loop for tests; production passes None.
  1774	        """
  1775	        if install_signals:
  1776	            self._install_signal_handlers()
  1777	        # Startup line names the loaded rule registry when the Tier-1 engine
  1778	        # is wired (Deploy AC: registry visible at startup). The engine also
  1779	        # logs its full rule list at construction.
  1780	        rule_names = getattr(self._engine, "rule_names", None)
  1781	        rules_desc = ",".join(rule_names()) if callable(rule_names) else "n/a"
  1782	        logger.info(
  1783	            "[pipeline_coordinator] entering steady state "
  1784	            "(engine=%s mode=%s tick=%.0fs heartbeat=%.0fs "
  1785	            "capacity_tracking=active capacity_path=%s rules=[%s])",
  1786	            self._engine.engine_version,
  1787	            self._mode_selector.select(),
  1788	            self._config.tick_interval_seconds,
  1789	            self._config.heartbeat_interval_seconds,
  1790	            self._config.capacity_path,
  1791	            rules_desc,
  1792	        )
  1793	        ticks = 0
  1794	        try:
  1795	            while not self._stop.is_set():
  1796	                self.run_once()
  1797	                ticks += 1
  1798	                if max_ticks is not None and ticks >= max_ticks:
  1799	                    break
  1800	                if self._stop.is_set():
  1801	                    break
  1802	                # Interruptible: returns True if stop fired while waiting.
  1803	                if self._sleep(self._config.tick_interval_seconds):
  1804	                    break
  1805	        finally:
  1806	            self._drain()
  1807	        return ticks
  1808	
  1809	    # ── L5 drain on shutdown (ADR-0021 §9 L5) ──
  1810	
  1811	    def _drain(self) -> None:
  1812	        """Write the shutdown markers and exit cleanly.
  1813	
  1814	        The skeleton has no in-flight LLM consultation to finish (§9 L5
  1815	        step 1 is a no-op here); it writes the ``shutdown_began`` /
  1816	        ``shutdown_complete`` pair (step 2) so L6 crash-recovery can tell a
  1817	        clean shutdown from a crash.
  1818	        """
  1819	        drain_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
  1820	        self._decision_log.append(
  1821	            {
  1822	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1823	                "event": "shutdown_began",
  1824	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1825	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  1826	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  1827	            }
  1828	        )
  1829	        # (29f: finish in-flight LLM consultation here, max-wait 60s.)
  1830	        self._decision_log.append(
  1831	            {
  1832	                "ts": self._clock().isoformat(),
  1833	                "event": "shutdown_complete",
  1834	                "engine_version": self._engine.engine_version,
  1835	                "decision_id": drain_id,
  1836	                "pid": os.getpid(),
  1837	            }
  1838	        )
  1839	        logger.info("[pipeline_coordinator] drain complete — exiting cleanly")
  1840	
  1841	    def _install_signal_handlers(self) -> None:
  1842	        if self._signals_installed:
  1843	            return
  1844	
  1845	        def _handler(signum: int, _frame: Any) -> None:
  1846	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] caught signal %d — draining", signum)
  1847	            self.request_stop()
  1848	
  1849	        for sig in (signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT):
  1850	            try:
  1851	                signal.signal(sig, _handler)
  1852	            except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
  1853	                # Non-main thread / unsupported platform — caller drives
  1854	                # stop via request_stop() instead.
  1855	                logger.debug(
  1856	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] could not install handler for %s: %s",
  1857	                    sig, exc,
  1858	                )
  1859	        self._signals_installed = True

   825	class ShadowColdStartGateway:
   826	    """Observe-only :class:`ColdStartGateway` for the 7-day shadow canary (OP-1555).
   827	
   828	    Wraps a real gateway and forwards every *read* (``audit_infra`` /
   829	    ``interrupted_tickets`` / ``stale_sweep_plan`` + the per-ticket probes) so
   830	    the 4-phase recovery still sees the true world — observation is the whole
   831	    point of shadow mode — but turns every *mutator* into a record-only no-op:
   832	    the would-be ``reset_to_todo`` / ``mark_resumable`` /
   833	    ``transition_under_review`` / ``remove_label`` / ``clear_assignee`` /
   834	    ``start_unit`` / ``mention_operator`` is logged (and lands in the decision
   835	    log via the orchestrator's per-phase ``startup_phase`` records) without
   836	    ever touching live JIRA / git / systemctl.
   837	
   838	    This is the cold-start sibling of :class:`ShadowActionExecutor` and of the
   839	    ``sprint_replan(shadow=not acting)`` wiring: the single ``acting`` switch in
   840	    :func:`build_default_coordinator` flips engine + sprint-replan + cold-start
   841	    together. Before OP-1555, ``build_default_coordinator`` wired the live
   842	    gateway unconditionally, so a coordinator deployed "in shadow mode"
   843	    (``acting=False``) still mutated live JIRA on every boot — the gap this
   844	    closes (sibling of OP-1552).
   845	    """
   846	
   847	    #: Marks this gateway observe-only (parallels ``ShadowActionExecutor.executed``).
   848	    acting: bool = False
   849	
   850	    def __init__(self, delegate: ColdStartGateway) -> None:
   851	        self._delegate = delegate
   852	
   853	    # ── reads: forwarded unchanged (observation is the point) ──
   854	    def audit_infra(self) -> InfraAuditResult:
   855	        return self._delegate.audit_infra()
   856	
   857	    def interrupted_tickets(self) -> list[InterruptedTicket]:
   858	        return self._delegate.interrupted_tickets()
   859	
   860	    def has_live_runner(self, key: str) -> bool:
   861	        return self._delegate.has_live_runner(key)
   862	
   863	    def gerrit_change_mergeable(self, key: str) -> bool:
   864	        return self._delegate.gerrit_change_mergeable(key)
   865	
   866	    def branch_has_commits(self, key: str) -> bool:
   867	        return self._delegate.branch_has_commits(key)
   868	
   869	    def stale_sweep_plan(self) -> StaleSweepPlan:
   870	        return self._delegate.stale_sweep_plan()
   871	
   872	    # ── mutators: record-only (never touch live JIRA / git / systemctl) ──
   873	    def start_unit(self, unit: str) -> bool:
   874	        # Record the would-be systemctl start and report success so the
   875	        # observe-only boot proceeds past Startup-1 to actually observe the
   876	        # reconcile + sweep phases, rather than halting on infra it was
   877	        # explicitly told not to touch.
   878	        self._record_shadow("start_unit", unit)
   879	        return True
   880
