   896	    def mention_operator(self, key: str, message: str, *, urgency: str = "high") -> None:
   897	        # An @-operator mention posts a JIRA comment + label — a live mutation,
   898	        # so it is gated too (the Deploy AC: no cold-start mutation path may
   899	        # fire while acting=False). The intent is still captured in the log.
   900	        self._record_shadow("mention_operator", f"{key} ({urgency})")
   901	
   902	    def _record_shadow(self, mutator: str, target: str) -> None:
   903	        logger.info(
   904	            "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start SHADOW (acting=False): recording "
   905	            "would-be %s(%s); not mutating live state.",
   906	            mutator,
   907	            target,
   908	        )
   909	
   910	
   920	
   921	    The script's ``DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG`` hook is used to get a
   922	    machine-readable row dump (rather than scraping the human table); each
   923	    ``systemd-unit`` / ``systemd-timer`` row maps to an :class:`InfraUnit`
   924	    (``live = status == "OK"``). The script exits non-zero when an
   925	    expected-live row is red — that's expected input here, not an error, so
   926	    the return code is ignored and only the JSONL is read.
   927	    """
   928	    repo_root = Path(repo_root) if repo_root is not None else _repo_root()
   929	    script = repo_root / "scripts" / "deployment-audit.sh"
   930	    if not script.exists():
   931	        logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] deployment-audit.sh not found at %s", script)
   932	        return InfraAuditResult()
   933	    import tempfile
   934	
   935	    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(
   936	        prefix="coord-audit-", suffix=".jsonl", delete=False
   937	    ) as tmp:
   938	        jsonl_path = Path(tmp.name)
   939	    try:
   940	        env = dict(os.environ)
   941	        env["DEPLOYMENT_AUDIT_JSONL_LOG"] = str(jsonl_path)
   942	        try:
   943	            subprocess.run(
   944	                ["bash", str(script)],
   945	                cwd=str(repo_root),
   946	                env=env,
   947	                capture_output=True,
   948	                text=True,
   949	                timeout=timeout_seconds,
   950	            )
   951	        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
   952	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] deployment-audit.sh run failed: %s", exc)
   953	            return InfraAuditResult()
   954	        record = _last_json_line(jsonl_path)
   955	        if not record:
   956	            return InfraAuditResult()
   957	        units: list[InfraUnit] = []
   958	        for row in record.get("rows", []):
   959	            if row.get("kind") in ("systemd-unit", "systemd-timer"):
   960	                units.append(
   961	                    InfraUnit(name=row.get("name", "?"), live=row.get("status") == "OK")
   962	                )
   963	        return InfraAuditResult(units=tuple(units))
   964	    finally:
   965	        try:
   966	            jsonl_path.unlink()
   967	        except OSError:
   968	            pass

   970	
   971	def _last_json_line(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   972	    try:
   973	        lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
   974	    except OSError:
   975	        return None
   976	    for line in reversed(lines):
   977	        if line.strip():
   978	            try:
   979	                rec = json.loads(line)
   980	            except json.JSONDecodeError:
   981	                return None
   982	            return rec if isinstance(rec, dict) else None
   983	    return None

  1023	        mode = ctx.mode
  1024	        if ctx.situation is not None:
  1025	            mode = self._mode_selector.select(ctx.situation, override=ctx.mode_override)

  1038	        # situation attached is a deliberate sprint-level/idle consult and is
  1039	        # allowed to proceed to Tier-2.

  1049	                tier=1,

   319	def evaluate_fast_forward(
   320	    *,
   321	    repo: Path,
   322	    source_branch: str,
   323	    target_branch: str,
   324	) -> PromotionResult:
   325	    """Return the promote pre-check shape for ``target <- source``.
   326	
   327	    OP-1554 (AUDIT-26d-followup): under ``MERGE_ALWAYS`` every prior
   328	    develop -> main promote leaves a merge commit on ``main``, so on the
   329	    second+ cut ``source_branch..target_branch`` (``main_only``) is
   330	    non-empty — the prior cut's merge commit(s). That is EXPECTED, not a
   331	    divergence, so it no longer gates. The only states are:
   332	
   333	    * ``noop`` — ``develop_only`` empty, nothing to promote;
   334	    * ``promotable`` — ``develop_only`` non-empty, REGARDLESS of
   335	      ``main_only``. The single promote path then builds one ``--no-ff``
   336	      merge commit; a TRUE divergence surfaces as a merge conflict
   337	      (:class:`MergeCommitConflict`), not as a pre-check refusal.
   338	
   339	    ``main_only`` stays on the result for observability/logging only.
   340	    """
   341	    develop_only = _git_lines(repo, "log", "--oneline", f"{target_branch}..{source_branch}")
   342	    main_only = _git_lines(repo, "log", "--oneline", f"{source_branch}..{target_branch}")
   343	    develop_tip = _git_one(repo, "rev-parse", source_branch)
   344	    main_tip = _git_one(repo, "rev-parse", target_branch)
   345	    status = "promotable" if develop_only else "noop"
   346	    return PromotionResult(
   347	        status=status,
   348	        version="",
   349	        develop_tip=develop_tip,
   350	        main_tip=main_tip,
   351	        develop_only=develop_only,
   352	        main_only=main_only,
   353	    )
   354	
   355	
   368	def _build_promote_merge_commit(
   369	    repo: Path,
   370	    develop_sha: str,
   371	    main_sha: str,
   372	    release_version: str,
   373	    meta_ticket: str,
   374	) -> str:
   375	    """Create a merge commit with parents ``[main_sha, develop_sha]``.
   376	
   377	    Subject: ``[release-cut {release_version}] Merge develop into main for {meta_ticket}``
   378	    Body: ``Develop tip: {develop_sha} • RELEASE META: {meta_ticket} • Reviewed at R8``
   379	    Returns merge commit SHA. Caller pushes to ``refs/for/main``.
   380	    """
   381	    subject = f"[release-cut {release_version}] Merge develop into main for {meta_ticket}"
   382	    body = f"Develop tip: {develop_sha} • RELEASE META: {meta_ticket} • Reviewed at R8"
   383	    try:
   384	        _git(repo, "checkout", "--detach", main_sha)
   385	        _git(
   386	            repo,
   387	            "merge",
   388	            "--no-ff",
   389	            "--no-edit",
   390	            "-m",
   391	            subject,
   392	            "-m",
   393	            body,
   394	            develop_sha,
   395	        )
   396	        return _git_one(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
   397	    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as exc:
   524	    target_ref = f"refs/for/{target_branch}"
   525	    review_ref = f"{merge_sha}:{target_ref}"
   526	    promote_topic = topic or promote_topic_for_version(version)
   527	    push = push_for_review(
   528	        repo=repo,
   529	        remote=remote,
   530	        commit_sha=merge_sha,
   531	        target_ref=target_ref,
   532	        hashtags=hashtags,
   533	        topic=promote_topic,
   534	        change_description=warning_block,
   535	    )

exec
/bin/bash -lc 'git status --short && git branch --show-current' in /home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer
 succeeded in 109ms:
 M audit/image_promotion_audit.jsonl
?? audit/promotion-predicates/
?? docs/audit/codex-reviews/op-1608-sora-bridge-repoint-codex-audit-2026-05-22.txt
?? docs/operations/2026-05-22-op-1608-sora-bridge-repoint-plan.md
develop

codex
**Findings**

- **BLOCKER - Bridge restart can become a stale-heartbeat wedge if develop lacks DB env.**  
  Develop bridge boot now enters `_run_with_db_pool()` before `stream_forever()` writes the first heartbeat. It raises fatal when no Postgres DSN is present, before heartbeat creation: [backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py:2423), heartbeat is only touched later in `stream_forever()`: [backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py:2184). The checked-in bridge unit loads only the webhook env file, not `.env` or `release-audit.env`: [deploy/systemd/gerrit-jira-bridge.service](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/deploy/systemd/gerrit-jira-bridge.service:94). If no deployed drop-in supplies `OMNISIGHT_DATABASE_URL`, the service will restart forever and runners will block on `bridge_down`: [auto-runner-jira.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/auto-runner-jira.py:486). The plan must preflight the *effective systemd env* for bridge, not just the checkout.

- **BLOCKER - Do not restart auto-promote; the cursor mitigation does not apply to the deployed path.**  
  `auto-promote-develop.service` is a oneshot wrapper, not the cursor-following `auto_promote_main.py` daemon path. The wrapper reads the latest milestone record by scanning the whole log and ignores `auto-promote.cursor`: [scripts/auto_promote_develop_to_main.sh](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/scripts/auto_promote_develop_to_main.sh:113). If the latest recognized event is `milestone_ready` or force-promoted, it calls `promote_on_milestone_ready()`: [scripts/auto_promote_develop_to_main.sh](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/scripts/auto_promote_develop_to_main.sh:131). That can build a merge commit and push `refs/for/main`: [backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py:527). It also checks out detached `main_sha` and does not restore the branch: [backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py:384). Deployment audit already marks both auto-promote paths retired under ADR-0040: [scripts/deployment-audit.sh](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/scripts/deployment-audit.sh:267). The plan should disable/leave it stopped, not restart it.

- **SHOULD-FIX - Coordinator watchdog can race cold-start before the first coordinator heartbeat.**  
  Coordinator only writes its heartbeat in `run_once()`: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1693), after `startup()` completes and `run_forever()` begins: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1675). Startup runs deployment audit plus JIRA/Gerrit probes. The watchdog restarts the service when heartbeat age is >=90s: [deploy/systemd/pipeline-coordinator-watchdog.service](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/deploy/systemd/pipeline-coordinator-watchdog.service:28). During a slow first develop boot, watchdog can repeatedly kill the coordinator before steady state. Pause watchdog or verify startup duration under 90s.

- **SHOULD-FIX - Coordinator JIRA mutation gate looks safe, but the plan should verify logs, not assume.**  
  The CLI builds with default `acting=False`: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1936). That wires `ShadowColdStartGateway`: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1896), whose mutators are record-only: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:872). OP-1556 idle-noop is present: [backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1029). Still, startup forwards reads to live JIRA, so acceptance should grep for `cold-start SHADOW` and zero JIRA write calls.

- **SHOULD-FIX - Sync self-heal only restarts the bridge.**  
  `sync_sora_bridge.sh` declares only five bridge-relevant files: [scripts/sync_sora_bridge.sh](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/scripts/sync_sora_bridge.sh:76), and only restarts `gerrit-jira-bridge.service`: [scripts/sync_sora_bridge.sh](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/scripts/sync_sora_bridge.sh:286). Future develop updates to coordinator, merger, or release scripts will update the checkout but not reload those services. The plan’s “all services running current develop code” is only true for the manual restart, not ongoing self-heal.

- **SHOULD-FIX - Rollback is incomplete if auto-promote or a failed merge has touched the checkout.**  
  `git reset --hard 2be0600` is enough only from a clean branch state. Auto-promote can leave `/home/user/sora-bridge` detached at a synthetic merge commit or in merge-conflict cleanup paths: [backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/auto_promote_main.py:384). Rollback should include `git status`, `git merge --abort || true`, explicit checkout/reset target, and service-specific stop/start order.

- **NICE - Heartbeat threshold in the plan is inconsistent with code/docs.**  
  The code default is 300s, not 60s: [backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/gerrit_jira_bridge.py:192), also documented in the runbook: [docs/sop/runbooks/bridge-health-degraded.md](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/sop/runbooks/bridge-health-degraded.md:140). If production has a 60s override, the plan should name the effective env source.

- **NICE - Git mechanics need explicit upstream verification.**  
  `git checkout -B develop origin/develop` is directionally right and preserves rc1 via remote/reflog, but the plan should explicitly verify `git branch -vv`, `git rev-parse HEAD`, `git rev-parse origin/develop`, and `git status --short` before service restarts.

I modified nothing. I did run `python3 -m py_compile` on the referenced Python modules; they compile in the current workspace.
